## Phase B9 — Microsoft 365 mail and calendar (D-48)

Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` (planner, Nov 18, 2026). Brief: D-48 – D-53, H-062 – H-065. Re-plans T-066 – T-068 (ids kept) and adds T-250 – T-269. Native Microsoft Graph behind the T-062 interfaces. **Since D-57 (Nov 21, 2026): Microsoft is an additional connector; one lane may have Google and Microsoft mail/calendar connections at the same time, permanently** (the enabled providers are a per-lane list in `lane_config`, T-269); nothing disconnects, hides or stops syncing a Google connection because Microsoft exists; there is no PKG cut-over (T-266 and H-071 rewritten). First live test Nichols Capital (D-50). Teams (D-52), the chat section (D-53) and OneDrive/SharePoint (T-187) are not part of this phase; Nichols Law is not enabled (D-51).

Standing rules for every task in this phase (not repeated below):
- App code (connectors, tokens, RLS, approvals): **full security + code review, fix all BLOCKER and HIGH** before done.
- MSW fakes of Entra (`login.microsoftonline.com`) and Graph with synthetic data only (rule 10). No live Microsoft call in any test. Each live check is a human item (H-070, H-072, H-073), never a task acceptance.
- Every SQL change: RLS + pgTAP including a cross-lane denial test in the same task (rule 3); every UPDATE/DELETE in a function has a top-level WHERE (pgTAP 315); production SQL as a gitignored numbered `supabase/core/bootstrap/manual-migrations/part-N-<task>.sql` (next free number after part 25) with `check-part-N.sql`, guarded, one transaction, refusing a second run.
- A Microsoft fact the task relies on that is marked "verify" in spec §15 is checked against Microsoft's current documentation first and the answer recorded in `docs/plan-review.md`; if it differs from the spec, stop and update the spec before coding.
- Google behaviour is unchanged: the existing Google tests pass without edits other than renamed imports.
- D-57: a lane may hold usable Google and Microsoft connections at the same time. Every job, reader, page and tool works on each connection by that connection row's own provider; no code path assumes "the lane's provider". Rows of both providers are shown together, each labelled with its provider and account.

- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §6.1, §6.2. `core.connections` gains `tenant_id uuid` and `cloud text` (`commercial` | `gcc_high`), allowed only on `provider = 'microsoft'` (check); set once by a new service-role security-definer function `core.connection_set_microsoft_identity(p_lane, p_connection_id, p_tenant_id, p_cloud)` (lane-checked, refuses a non-Microsoft row and a second set); `core.connections_identity_immutable` refuses any later change; the health columns grant includes both (no content). `core.oauth_state_create` / `core.oauth_state_consume` treat `microsoft` like `google` (own_mail); TypeScript `connectRolesFor("microsoft")` returns `MAIL_CONNECT_ROLES`. pgTAP: set-once, immutability, non-Microsoft refused, viewer may start a Microsoft state, finance_operator may not, QuickBooks still needs owner/admin_technical, cross-lane state and cross-lane identity set refused, AAL1 refused; catalog test updated; production part + check.

### T-252 SQL: provider-generic mail page writer
- status: done
- depends: T-063, T-254
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §6.3. New latest definition of `core.comms_mail_apply_page` (from the body in `20261113120000_message_flags_and_thread_ref.sql`, or a newer one if it exists): usable connection of provider `google` or `microsoft`, rows inserted with the connection's own provider (no `'google'` literal); cursor must start with `g1.<connection id>.` (google) or `m1.<connection id>.m:` (microsoft), at most 16384 characters, else 22023 (closes the T-063 re-review LOW); removals accept a string or `{message_id, folder}` (with a folder only a stored row in that folder is flagged deleted); on update an absent flag keeps the stored value, on insert it is false. TypeScript: `MessageRemovalSchema` gains optional `folder`; the mail store passes it. pgTAP: Microsoft page applied, Google regression unchanged, foreign-prefix cursor refused for each provider, folder-scoped removal, flag keep-on-update, cross-lane denial (a lane-B connection with lane A refused), disabled lane refused; production part + check.

- status: done

New latest definition of `core.cal_events_apply_page`: provider `google` or `microsoft`, prefix `g1.<id>.c:` or `m1.<id>.c:` by the row's provider; the inventory of every function and view whose latest body names `'google'` (start from the plan-review T-219 finding 1 list and the T-130 / T-200 / T-231a / T-238 / T-247 functions; search all migrations) recorded in `docs/plan-review.md` with a decision per item; "mail provider" meanings generalised to `google`/`microsoft` in the same migration; a pgTAP test lists those functions and asserts each accepts a Microsoft connection (mail and calendar readers, coverage, home calendar status, sync-now/job-failure streams) and still refuses another user's and another lane's rows (T-247 rules); production part + check.

### T-269 Lane config: several mail providers per lane (D-57 follow-up to T-250)
- status: done
- depends: T-250
- note: approved by Saurabh Nov 21, 2026 ("t269 approved").
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §4. TypeScript only, no SQL. (1) **Schema** (`packages/core/src/lane-config`): `mail` gains `providers: ("google" | "microsoft")[]` (1..2 entries, unique, default `["google"]`). The T-250 key `mail.provider` stays accepted as a deprecated input: when it is present and `providers` is absent it is read as the one-element list `[provider]`; a config with both keys is refused (ambiguous); the parsed config exposes only `mail.providers` (the legacy key is not re-emitted), so any version the app writes (lane config editor, T-047) carries `providers` only (test). (2) **Refines** (one definition, used by the whole schema and the reader's narrow view, replacing `refineMailProvider`): `microsoft` in `providers` requires the `microsoft` block; `mail_transport = "composio"` requires `google` in `providers` and applies to Google connections only (it is no longer refused because Microsoft is also enabled; Microsoft connections are always `native`); a `microsoft` block without `microsoft` in `providers` stays allowed (a lane prepared, or Microsoft paused, before or after use). (3) **Calendar ids per provider**: `calendar.calendar_ids` keeps its D-26 meaning, raw **Google** calendar ids only (the reader `calendarSettings` is unchanged); the `microsoft` block gains optional `calendar_ids` (raw Graph ids, 1..5 entries, unique, same character rule as the Google ids, secret scan applied), which replaces the default selection for Microsoft connections only (spec §9). Why per provider: the list replaces the default selection, so one shared list would leave the other provider's connections with no calendar or need a silent fallback. (4) **Reader** (`packages/connectors/src/vault/lane-config.ts`): `mailProviders(laneId)` (the list, default `["google"]` for a missing config or mail section) and `isMailProviderEnabled(laneId, provider)`; `mailProviderSettings(laneId)` returns `{ providers, microsoft }` from ONE read and stays the only accessor for allow decisions; `microsoftSettings(laneId)` returns the block with `calendarIds` (or null); every read fails closed (`provider_error`) on a malformed `mail.providers`, `mail.provider`, `mail_transport` or `microsoft`, and on both keys present. `mailProvider(laneId)` is **removed**; its only callers are `packages/connectors/src/vault/lane-config.test.ts` (checked Nov 21, 2026: no app or connector code calls `mailProvider`, `microsoftSettings` or `mailProviderSettings` yet); the builder re-checks with a repo-wide search and lists every caller changed in the PR body. Comments in `packages/core/src/lane-config/index.ts` that say "one provider per lane" / "before its cut-over" are corrected. (5) **Tests** (core and connectors): a stored PKG-style config with no provider keys reads `["google"]`; a stored T-250-style config `mail.provider = "microsoft"` + `microsoft` block reads `["microsoft"]`, and `mail.provider = "google"` reads `["google"]`; both enabled (`["google","microsoft"]` + block) parses and `isMailProviderEnabled` is true for each; `["microsoft"]` without the block refused; `["google","microsoft"]` with `mail_transport = "composio"` accepted; `["microsoft"]` with `composio` refused; empty list, duplicates, unknown provider, a non-array, and both keys present refused; `microsoft.calendar_ids` limits and secret scan; the reader fails closed on each malformed shape, reads on every call, and returns providers and Microsoft settings from one read (one query counted).

### T-255 Provider-agnostic comms wiring (facade and generic sync runners)
- status: done
- depends: T-250, T-252, T-253, T-269
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §4, §8.3, §8.6. `createMailSync` / `createCalendarSync` and the mail/calendar index stores move out of `google/` into `comms/` and are generic over the provider (log names by provider); `isMailAddress` moves out of `google/headers` so `comms/schemas` no longer imports Google code; a server-only facade in `packages/connectors` dispatches `readBody`, `search`, `prepareEmailWrite`, the sync runners and the email writer/reconciler by the **authoritative connection row's provider** (the T-247 owner check kept), never by a lane-level value; apps/web callers (`readGmailBody`, `prepareEmailWrite`, the reply route, briefing body reads, `mail-ingest-delta` and `cal-ingest-delta` target lists, `CALENDAR_PROVIDERS`, `MAIL_PROVIDERS` in `sync/targets.ts`) use it; the target lists return every usable connection of both providers, so one lane's tick runs all its usable Google **and** Microsoft connections; before any token read each sync and read checks `isMailProviderEnabled(laneId, row.provider)` through `mailProviderSettings` (one read) and skips a connection whose provider is not enabled for the lane (reported `provider_disabled`, no external call, nothing deleted, status unchanged); a Microsoft connection in a lane with `microsoft: null` is skipped the same way (T-250 review L1); the Google OAuth start/callback refuses when `google` is not enabled for the lane (no change for any stored lane: the default is `["google"]`); Microsoft entries are registered but answer `unsupported_op` until their tasks land; `describeCommsContract` gains the options a Graph provider needs (`removedEventsAreStubs: false`). Tests: Google regression suite unchanged; dispatch by provider; one lane with a Google and a Microsoft connection of the same user in one tick (both dispatched, each to its own runner); a lane with `["google"]` holding a Microsoft row (the Microsoft row skipped `provider_disabled` before any token read, the lane's Google rows synced); a lane with `["microsoft"]` holding a Google row (the reverse); the TypeScript index twin (`testing/google-harness.ts`) accepts both providers (T-252 review note).

### T-256 Microsoft: Graph HTTP client, token manager, guard and health
- status: done
- depends: T-251, T-255, T-034
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §5.3, §7 (Tokens, Guard), §8.5. `packages/connectors/src/microsoft/` with `import "server-only"` entry points; host allow-list per cloud (`commercial` only here; `gcc_high` hosts refused until T-267); `createGraphHttp` refuses other hosts before a token is read; per-connection rate bucket and at most 4 concurrent requests per mailbox, separate `:write` bucket, `Retry-After` honoured on 429/503 through the shared client; token manager mirroring Google's (refresh under 5 min or on 401, single-flight, compare-and-swap `rotateToken` because refresh tokens rotate (verify V-8), loser re-reads, refresh never retried, `invalid_grant` and the consent-revoked / conditional-access family → `reconnect_required`); guard on every call, from one `mailProviderSettings` read (lane brand, provider `microsoft`, authoritative row, usable status, **Microsoft enabled for the lane** = `microsoft` in `mail.providers` with valid `microsoft` settings (a lane with `microsoft: null` refuses), row `tenant_id` in `tenant_ids`, row `cloud` = lane cloud, mail policy on the address); the guard does **not** look at the lane's Google connections or Google settings, and a lane with Google enabled as well is no reason to refuse; health check `GET /me` registered with `conn.token_health`; error map (400/401/403/404/410/429/5xx/timeouts → retry, `reconnect_required` or failed-with-message; no provider text stored, T-196 error log used); tests incl. token canary (never in logs, errors or responses), a lost refresh race, 429 storm on one tenant not delaying another, a tenant removed from config refused before any call, `microsoft` removed from `mail.providers` refused before any call (block kept), and a lane with `["google","microsoft"]` accepted.

### T-066 Microsoft: Entra connect (multi-tenant) and identity
- status: done
- depends: T-256, T-254, T-035
- note: builds on fakes with no human item; the live checks are H-062 (app registration), H-066 (test tenant) and H-070.
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §5, §7. `OAuthProvider` `microsoft` (slug `microsoft`, callback `/api/connect/microsoft/callback`, PKCE, client secret in the body); `endpoints(laneId)` from the lane's cloud with authority = the single tenant id or `organizations`, never `common`; scopes `openid offline_access User.Read Mail.Read Calendars.Read`, plus `Mail.ReadWrite Mail.Send` only when the lane policy allows email writes (`scopesFor`, same switch as Google's compose); start and callback order of spec §7: precheck from one `mailProviderSettings` read (domains configured; Microsoft enabled for the lane = `microsoft` in `mail.providers` and the `microsoft` settings present and valid; never "the lane's provider is microsoft"; a lane that also has Google enabled and Google connections is accepted, and those Google rows are not read, changed or disconnected) → exchange → exact granted scopes (handles the `scope` format, V-5) and a refresh token → `tid` from the id_token in `tenant_ids` else new result `wrong_tenant` → GCC check per spec §5.4 (V-3; if not detectable, the gate is documented) → `GET /me` `mail` required, no guest → mail policy → reconnect same address and tenant else `wrong_account` → D-23 and ownership (existing) → Vault store → `core.connection_set_microsoft_identity` → features `graph_mail_write` → audit; known consent errors mapped to new result `admin_consent_required` (V-7), others `denied`; `login_hint` on reconnect; a refused grant is never stored and shows `GRANT_NOT_STORED_MESSAGE`. Tests against an MSW fake Entra + Graph `/me` for every refusal in spec §17 tests 4 – 9, plus "no tenant ids configured refuses before the exchange", "personal-account tenant refused", "Microsoft not enabled for the lane (no `microsoft` block, or `microsoft` not in `mail.providers`) refuses at start, nothing stored, the lane's Google connect still works", and "a user who already has an active Google connection with the same address in the lane connects the Microsoft mailbox; both rows active" (different provider, D-23 index is per provider).

### T-257 Settings: "Your Microsoft accounts", admin consent help and local Disconnect
- status: done
- depends: T-066, T-199
- needs: D-55
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §5.5, §7. On Settings → Connections, "Your Google accounts" and "Your Microsoft accounts" are shown **side by side**, each when that provider is enabled for the lane (`isMailProviderEnabled`, T-269); the existing Google section is otherwise unchanged. "Your Microsoft accounts": connect, reconnect per row, several accounts per user, for members with `own_mail`; other people's rows as health only for owner/admin_technical (D-43). A connection whose provider is not (or no longer) enabled for the lane is listed as "Paused: <Google|Microsoft> is not enabled for this company" with no Connect or Reconnect, its data kept (nothing deleted; Disconnect stays available under that provider's existing rule); there is no "this company now uses <provider>" note anywhere. The admin consent note with a link built from the client id, the lane's tenant id, the cloud and the registered redirect URI (no secret, no state stored); fixed messages for `admin_consent_required`, `wrong_tenant`, `wrong_account`, `account_refused`; Disconnect per D-55 (mailbox owner only, AAL2, confirm, Vault tokens deleted, row `disconnected`, audited, instructions to remove the app at Microsoft); wording says this connects mail and calendar read-only and is not how anyone signs in. Server actions return 403 to other roles and 404 to other lanes (tests); tests also cover a lane with both providers enabled (both sections, the same user's Google and Microsoft rows each in its own section), a lane with only Google (no Microsoft section, Connect Microsoft refused server-side), and a lane where Microsoft was disabled after connect (row shown paused, no Reconnect); Vitest + a Playwright spec with the MSW fakes (run by a human if the e2e run would reset the local database).

### T-258 Microsoft: mail delta sync (metadata)
- status: done
- depends: T-066, T-252, T-255
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §8.1, §8.2, §8.5. `MailProvider<"microsoft">.sync`: folders from `microsoft.mail_folders` resolved by well-known name; per-folder `messages/delta` with `$select` of index fields only, `Prefer: IdType="ImmutableId"` (the fake refuses a request without it) and `odata.maxpagesize`; first load bounded by 90 days or the lane's retention (V-10); composite cursor `m1.<connection id>.m:<base64url JSON>` with folder ids, next/delta links, position and a settings fingerprint; size test of the largest allowed state under 16 KB (V-12); one page per call; `@removed` → `{message_id, folder}` removals; 410 / sync-state errors and foreign or malformed cursors → `fullResyncRequired`; field mapping of spec §8.2 (canonical `INBOX`/`SENT`/`ARCHIVE`, `SENT` label, `OTHER`, `cat:` categories, `webLink` host check per cloud, V-15); time budget per run (`hasMore`); mail policy re-checked per call; `describeCommsContract` passes. Tests: spec §17 tests 11 – 15 and 17 with the fake Graph, and a Microsoft sync in a lane that also holds a Google connection of the same user (the Google rows and cursor untouched).

### T-260 Microsoft mail in the jobs, Home, Inbox and briefing
- status: done
- depends: T-259, T-238
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §8.6, §13. `mail-ingest-delta` and `conn.sync` run the Microsoft connections of every lane where Microsoft is enabled, in the same tick as that lane's Google connections, through the facade; Inngest concurrency per connection plus per tenant (`tenant_id`) for Microsoft rows (Google keys unchanged); Coverage, Sync now, the stale line and the failure alerts treat Microsoft streams like Google's and list both providers' connections of a lane together; Home ("Needs your attention"), Inbox and the briefing show the user's Google and Microsoft mail **together in one list**, each row labelled with its provider and account (mailbox address), with "Open in Gmail" or "Open in Outlook" per row from that row's `source_url` and provider names taken from the row's connection (no hard-coded "Gmail" in shared UI); no de-duplication across providers (D-58 default); the reply drawer and briefing body reads go through the facade with the owner check and use the row's own connection. Tests: a lane with two Microsoft mailboxes of two users (each sees only their own); a lane where one user has a Google and a Microsoft mailbox (both synced in one tick, both shown in Home/Inbox/briefing, each labelled, the right "Open in" link per row, the same message present in both shown twice); a mixed tick across lanes (a Google-only lane and a lane with both); UI text per provider.

### T-067 Microsoft: calendar delta sync
- status: done
- depends: T-066, T-253, T-255
- needs: D-59
- acceptance: Note (Nov 21, 2026, D-59 decided: default calendar only): where this acceptance and D-59 differ, D-59 governs. Sync ONLY the person's default calendar with `GET /me/calendarView/delta?startDateTime=..&endDateTime=..` (the form Microsoft documents for v1.0); no `GET /me/calendars` selection, no per-calendar delta, no "at most 5 calendars"; `microsoft.calendar_ids` and `calendar.include_read_only` are not applied to a Microsoft connection (a configured `microsoft.calendar_ids` is ignored and logged once as a count, never an error); the cursor holds one link and its window; `readBody` reads an event of the default calendar only; everything else below (window, mapping, removals, bodies dropped in memory, contract suite, size test, spec §17 tests 18 – 19) stands. Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §9. `CalendarProvider<"microsoft">`: discovery `GET /me/calendars` with the D-26-equivalent selection (default + owned/editable; read-only only with `include_read_only`; `microsoft.calendar_ids` (T-269) replaces; `calendar.calendar_ids` holds Google ids and is never applied to a Microsoft connection), at most 5 calendars (the rest counted, never named), settings fingerprint re-checked per page; `calendarView/delta` per calendar over retention-back to `forward_days`-ahead windows (V-17); cursor `m1.<connection id>.c:` with per-calendar links and window, reload at half horizon; mapping of spec §9 (series/occurrences, `isCancelled`, https-only join URL, host-checked `webLink`, attendees capped, all-day with the original zone mapped to IANA or skipped and logged, V-18); `@removed` → `removals`; bodies in delta pages dropped in memory (canary test: not in store, logs or errors); `readBody` for a selected calendar only; contract suite passes with `removedEventsAreStubs: false`; size test for the largest allowed cursor. Tests: spec §17 tests 18 – 19.

- needs: D-34
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §10. `prepareWrite` for `email.save_draft` / `email.send` (no external call; sender = the connection's mailbox; `inReplyTo.threadId` = conversation id); a Microsoft email writer exported only through `@nichols/connectors/write` with the checks of spec §10 (incl. `graph_mail_write`, owner = approver, open claim); Save as draft = `createReply` in the approved conversation (parent read first) or a new message, then PATCH of exactly the approved recipients, subject and body with the `X-Portal-Action-Id` header and the extended-property marker (V-19); marker lookup in Drafts before any create and after an ambiguous failure; POSTs never retried; read-back verify; write-client allow-list of exactly the spec §10 method + path templates (a test enumerates DELETE, move, copy, forward, rules and settings and expects refusal before a token read); lint/source test that only approvals imports the writer; in a lane with both providers, a reply drafted from a Microsoft row is prepared against that Microsoft connection and one from a Google row against its Google connection (the provider comes from the row's connection, never from the lane). ### T-068 Microsoft: change notifications (subscriptions and receiver)
- status: done
- depends: T-260, T-261, T-036, T-040
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §6.4, §14. `core.push_subscriptions` (lane_id, RLS health-only, definer writers, retention entry, pgTAP incl. cross-lane denial); `core.webhook_events` accepts provider `microsoft` (extend the check if needed, pgTAP); `watch`/`stop` for mail (inbox) and each synced calendar of Microsoft connections in lanes where Microsoft is enabled (a lane's Google watch, T-065, is independent and unchanged), `notificationUrl`/`lifecycleNotificationUrl` from server config only, `clientState` a random Vault secret per subscription; `POST /api/webhooks/microsoft`: validation token echoed as `text/plain` and nothing else, `clientState` compared in constant time, unknown subscription or wrong `clientState` dropped, deduped in `core.webhook_events`, each valid notification only queues a debounced delta sync for its connection (no inline content read, no rich notifications); `capabilities.watch` becomes `push` with polling kept as fallback. ### T-264 Microsoft: subscription renewal and lifecycle events
- status: done
- depends: T-068
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §14. Microsoft renewer in the T-040 `conn.subscriptions_renew` registry renews subscriptions expiring within 24 h (expiry within Graph's maximum, V-21); lifecycle `reauthorizationRequired` → renew, `subscriptionRemoved` → recreate, `missed` → delta sync; a renewal that fails falls back to polling and emits `conn/attention.needed` (`subscription_renewal_failed`); renew/stop act only on subscriptions recorded for the same (lane, connection); a disconnected connection's subscriptions are stopped, and so are those of a connection whose provider is no longer enabled for the lane (paused; nothing else changes and no Google subscription or watch is touched by Microsoft renewals). Tests with fake clocks and the fake Graph. Human step: H-073.

### T-267 GCC High cloud (only if needed)
- status: approved
- depends: T-066, T-258, T-067
- needs: H-064, H-065, D-49
- acceptance: Spec: `docs/specs/microsoft.md` §5.2 – §5.4. ### T-270 Fixes from the first live Microsoft run (production lane `NC`, Nov 22, 2026)
- status: done
- depends: T-265, T-260, T-261
- note: approved by Saurabh Nov 22, 2026 ("approved"). Source: `docs/plan-review.md`, "First live Microsoft run, production lane `NC`", faults (a) to (f).
- acceptance: Generic for every lane and both providers; a lane with Google only behaves as before unless stated. (a) **AI summary and sources:** no automatic or manual summary run, and no model call, for a person who has no usable own mail or calendar connection in the lane (Home shows the existing "connect a mailbox" state instead of a generated summary); a summary prepared before the person's newest connection finished its first successful sync counts as stale, so the next Home load renews it once (the daily cap and one-run-at-a-time rules still hold). (b) **Coverage:** the Mail row shows a sync time and "OK" only after a mail sync of that connection has stored its position; before that it shows "not synced yet" (same for Calendar); the time of the other stream is never shown. (c) **Sync now on Settings** whenever the person may sync and the lane has at least one usable mail or calendar connection of any provider (today it is drawn only inside the Google section); one button, not one per provider. (d) **"Waiting on you" for automated mail:** find why an automated report in a Microsoft mailbox is tagged "Waiting on you" and fix it with a provider-neutral rule built on documented header signals already read at sync (for example `Auto-Submitted`, `Precedence`, `List-*`), never on a sender or company name in code; if no such rule separates it, change nothing and report what was found. (e) **Diagnosable refusals:** when a connect is refused because of granted scopes, the log line names the unexpected scope(s), each only if it matches a short safe pattern (letters, digits, dot, dash, underscore; capped in count and length; anything else is logged as `other`), and never a token, code, id_token or address; `[api/connect/start] unavailable` logs the ConnectorError kind and its fixed message. (f) **Runbook `docs/runbooks/enable-lane-mail.md`:** the redirect address is `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` + the callback path (never a domain that is not live), the exact Graph permissions the Entra app may have and how to revoke then remove any other, where the first mail sync time comes from (mail and calendar run on different quarter-hours), and how to read the refusal log line. Tests for each item, with a Microsoft-only lane and a Google-only lane; if any SQL changes: RLS kept, pgTAP incl. cross-lane denial, and a production part with its check file in the same change.

Change the order of the sections to: 1. Home 2. Inbox 3. Calendar 4. Claude 5. ChatGPT 6. Finance 7. Tasks 8. Contacts. And also include messages. These messages will be the Teams connection, which will be doing it, but for now, just make this on the left sidebar. The settings will remain there as it is, the way it is now."
- today: the sidebar is one list for every lane (`packages/ui/src/lib/nav.ts`): Get started / AI brain, Home, Finance, Inbox, Calendar, Approvals, Assistant, Projects, Settings. iPhone bottom tabs: Home, Approvals, Inbox, Ask, More.
- open questions (asked Nov 23, 2026; fill in his answers here before approval):
  1. Claude: is it today's Assistant renamed, or something new?

We are just embedding it. We are not doing anything more around that." So Claude and ChatGPT are two new pages that embed the vendors' own chat interfaces; no portal chat is built for them. To check before building: claude.ai and chatgpt.com normally refuse to load inside another site's frame, so the embed method (a third-party tool, or a link that opens the site) has to be found first. Questions 3 to 6 are still open.
- more answers (Saurabh, Nov 23, 2026): "And get the 'Get Started' option from the top in the settings. You just need to keep it clean, and this menu is for all the companies." So: Get started / AI brain leaves the top of the sidebar and is reached from Settings; the sidebar is one list for every company (question 5 answered: no per-company order). Still open: Approvals and today's Assistant (keep, move or remove), whether Tasks is the Projects page renamed, where Messages goes, and the iPhone bottom tabs.
- final answers (Saurabh, Nov 23, 2026): "Approvals: you can keep it under Inbox, maybe a separate subsection under Inbox for Approvals and Assistant. For now, you can just keep it under Settings. We will figure it out: where do we have to put it? The tasks: they are the various projects, so you can rename that to Tasks. Messages: yes, right after Contacts. iPhone bottom tabs: just match them with what we have just discussed." Read as: Approvals and Assistant are opened from Settings for now (a sub-section under Inbox is the idea for later; their final place is undecided); Projects is renamed Tasks; Messages follows Contacts; the phone tabs follow the new menu. Building started on these answers ("let me know if you have any questions"; none were left).

Get started / AI brain, Approvals and Assistant have no menu entry; they keep their pages and are opened from Settings, and Settings is the selected entry while one is open. iPhone tabs: Home, Inbox, Calendar, Claude, More; More lists the rest of the menu. A company's lane_config can still hide a module it does not use (T-265). No company-specific code. Tests for the order, the selected entry, the phone tabs, the Settings links and the new pages.

### T-273 fflate 0.8.2 to 0.8.3 (AI Brain .zip import could freeze the browser tab)
- status: done
- depends: none
- note: Approved by Saurabh on Nov 23, 2026 ("yes, do the fflate bump"). Found by `pnpm audit` during T-272 (GHSA-px8p-9vwx-vf98) and traced in the T-272 security review: the AI Brain .zip is read in the person's browser (`apps/web/lib/import-files.ts`, streaming `Unzip`); a crafted file with a ZIP64 size marker and no ZIP64 extra field made fflate 0.8.2 loop forever before any of the import's limits ran. Impact: a frozen tab for the person importing; no server or data impact.
- acceptance: `fflate` in `apps/web` is pinned to 0.8.3 or later and the lockfile holds no older copy; a test feeds the crafted header to `readBrainZip` and gets "unreadable" at once (guarded so it cannot hang the run on an older fflate); `pnpm audit` lists no `fflate` advisory; `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test` and the web build pass.

### T-274 Calendar page: the person's own Google and Microsoft calendars together
- status: done
- depends: T-130, T-067, T-247, T-271
- note: Requested by Saurabh on Nov 23, 2026, as the next interface change after T-271. His words, verbatim: "in the calendar section on the left side...can we have the calendar integrated for the accounts signed in...whether google or microsoft". Read as: the Calendar page (a placeholder until now) shows the events of every Google and Microsoft account the signed-in person has connected in the company. Built on this request (standing instruction: keep building; he tests after). Choices made by the agent, for him to change if he wants: read-only (creating or changing events stays with the approvals work, T-181); Week, Month and Day views; times in Eastern Time as on Home; weeks start on Sunday; the same meeting on two of the person's calendars is shown once (as Home does today; D-58 stays open).
- today: `/[laneId]/calendar` is the placeholder page (`ModulePage`). Events of both providers are already synced into `cal.event_index` (T-130, T-067), private to the mailbox owner (T-247), and read on Home through `core.home_meetings` (7 days at most, no account label).
- acceptance: Generic for every company and both providers; no company-specific code. (a) **Data:** a new read-only SQL function returns the caller's own events of one lane for a window long enough for a month grid, from every Google and Microsoft connection the caller owns there, with the provider and the connection(s) each event came from; cancelled events left out; the same meeting on several of the caller's calendars returned once; another person's events, another lane's events and a session without MFA return nothing (pgTAP, including cross-lane and cross-user denial); no event description is read or stored (rule 6). (b) **Page:** Day, Week and Month views with Today / previous / next, the view and date in the URL; timed events placed by time with overlapping events side by side, all-day events in their own row; each event shows its time, title and which account it belongs to (one colour per account, with a legend that names provider and address); opening an event shows time, place, organizer, the account, "Open in Google Calendar" or "Open in Outlook", and "Join" for an https meeting link (never opened automatically). On a phone the default is the Day view and the Week view is a list by day. (c) **States:** no calendar connected (a link to Settings to connect Google or Microsoft), no access, an account that needs reconnecting (named, with a link to Settings), and "could not load" (never an empty calendar on an error). (d) Text from events is shown as plain text; links are https only. (e) Tests: pgTAP for the function; unit tests for the date and layout rules including the two daylight-saving days; server render tests for each view and each state with synthetic events; the directive-exports check passes. Production SQL as a manual part with a check.

### T-275 Connecting a second Google account answers "unavailable" (production PKG)
- status: done
- merged: PR #69, Nov 23, 2026 (the code part). H-076 closed the same day: the variable is corrected and two Google accounts are connected.
- depends: T-247
- needs: H-076 (Saurabh corrects the production variable) for the live result; the code part is built
- note: Reported by Saurabh on Nov 23, 2026, verbatim: "when I click on connect another account for google...I want to connect saurabh@pubkgroup.com too...I am getting error \"error\": \"unavailable\" } both saurabh@pub-k.org and saurabh@pubkgroup.com are part of the same google workspace thoguh". Found so far (code read, not reproduced): the bare JSON answer comes from the connect routes themselves (`packages/connectors/src/oauth/handlers.ts`), not from the Settings result message, so it is an unexpected error on the start step or in the session check of the callback, not a refusal of the account (a refused account, a wrong domain or a missing setting each have their own message on Settings). The start step is the same for a first and a second account, so a first connect would most likely fail the same way today. To check once the log line is known: the lane settings read at start (`providerGate`, the compose flag), `core.oauth_state_create` in production, the audit write, `GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI`. Separate question to settle with him: if pubkgroup.com is a domain ALIAS of pub-k.org (not a separate user), `saurabh@pubkgroup.com` is the same Google account and mailbox as `saurabh@pub-k.org`; Google then reports the primary address and there is no second account to connect.
- cause (Nov 23, 2026, from the log line, H-075): the production variable `GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI` does not end with `/api/connect/google/callback`, so the Google provider refuses to start any connect (first or second account alike). The fix is the variable (H-076). Code change in this task: that refusal (any provider's `<NAME>_REDIRECT_URI must end with <path>`) now sends a person's Connect click back to Settings with the existing "isn't configured on this server" banner instead of bare JSON; scripts keep the JSON 503. Still open after H-076: whether the second account connects, and the alias question.
- acceptance: the cause is named from the production log line; the fix (code or a production setting) lets a member connect a second allowed Google account of their own, with a test that fails before the fix; an unexpected error on the start step of a connect button sends the person back to Settings with a readable message instead of bare JSON; if the two addresses turn out to be one Google account, Settings says so instead of failing.

### T-276 Faster navigation: menu clicks and page loads
- status: done
- merged: PR #70, Nov 23, 2026. Not measured in production; H-077 (Supabase region, one `SERVER_TIMING` measurement) stays open if it still feels slow.
- depends: T-230, T-271
- note: Reported by Saurabh on Nov 23, 2026, verbatim: "also the app loads slowly...clicking on the menus and navigating shud be more faster". T-230 (Nov 9) did the first speed pass (request-scoped caches, skeletons, prefetch, `SERVER_TIMING` opt-in). Since then: Cache Components (T-243), the module gate (T-265), per-user mail (T-247), Microsoft, the new menu (T-271) and the Calendar page (T-274). Measure first, then fix.
- audit (Nov 23, 2026; read-only, from the code and the last build output; nothing measured): database round trips per menu click today, and how many run one after another: Home about 35 (5 to 8 levels; 18 calls / 5 levels at T-230), Inbox 5 (5 levels; was 3 / 2), Calendar 6 (5 levels), Finance 7 (4 levels), Tasks 3 (3 levels), Settings about 30 to 36 (6 to 7 levels; was 17 / 4), Claude / ChatGPT / Contacts / Messages 2 (1 level). Causes since T-230: the module gate (T-265) waits before the page starts its own reads; Home reads the lane settings 5 times and the coverage twice, starts Finance late and waits for the Microsoft reply check; Settings waits for its longest chain before showing anything; the four new placeholder pages have no skeleton of their own and are fully dynamic; a typed address or reload shows an empty page until the layout's reads finish. Not built here, listed for Saurabh: skipping the session check for menu prefetches in the proxy (changes when cookies refresh; security decision), keeping a visited page for N seconds on revisit (figures could be N seconds old), the production settings in H-077.
- acceptance: (1) a written count, per menu page, of the auth and database round trips one navigation makes today and which run one after another, with the regressions since T-230 named; (2) the fixes that need no product decision are made: nothing weakens a security check; repeated session / lane / gate reads in one request are shared; independent reads run in parallel; menu links prefetch; every menu page shows its skeleton at once; (3) anything that needs Saurabh is listed with exact steps (for example the Vercel function region against the Supabase region, turning on `SERVER_TIMING` for one measurement); (4) before and after counts of round trips and sequential levels per page, from tests that count them (a timed local run needs the e2e reset, which is not allowed; hosted timings come from H-077 step 4); tests for the shared reads.

### T-277 Messages: spec for Teams chat in the portal (see, reply, send, call)
- status: approved
- depends: T-271, T-066, T-067, T-111
- decisions: D-62 (a Teams sign-in of its own, also in a company whose mail is Google), D-63 (calling inside the portal), D-64 (chats and team channels), D-65 (Send = your approval), all answered by Saurabh Nov 23, 2026.
- note: Requested by Saurabh on Nov 23, 2026, verbatim: "now can we also integrate teams for both google and microsoft accounts? user shud be ab;le to see, reply, send messages and make calls". A new module, so the planner writes `docs/specs/messages.md` and the build tasks first (CLAUDE.md step 4). Known from the code and docs today: the Messages page is a placeholder (T-271); the Microsoft connection asks only for mail and calendar permissions (`MICROSOFT_READ_SCOPES`, `MICROSOFT_WRITE_SCOPES`), so Teams needs new permissions on the Entra app, admin consent per tenant and a reconnect by each person; message text may not be stored (rule 6: list details stored, text fetched live); a sent message is an external write and must go through approvals (rule 5); GCC High tenants need T-267 first. Not verified against the vendors' current documentation yet (the planner does that): the exact Microsoft Graph permissions for reading and sending chats, how new messages are noticed (polling or change notifications), and whether a call can be started for the person from a web page (expected: only by opening Teams with a link, or with Azure Communication Services).
- note (Nov 23, 2026): Saurabh asked, verbatim: "cant we embed entire teams into the app?". Answer from Microsoft's documentation (`.agent/notes/T-277-vendor-facts.md` §F): no; there is no supported way to show the Teams client inside another site's page, Microsoft sign-in refuses to render in a frame, and the Graph Toolkit chat component was retired on 2026-08-28; the documented routes are our own chat/channel pages over Graph, links that open Teams, or the portal as a tab inside Teams (open question Q-M12, not a task). He then chose option 1 (D-63 revised): our own Messages page, calls open Teams.
- acceptance: `docs/specs/messages.md` with the data model (lane, owner, RLS, pgTAP), the connector permissions and consent steps, read / reply / send through approvals, calls, the Messages page and phone layout, tests, the human setup items, and the build tasks added to this backlog as `proposed` for Saurabh's approval.
- result (planner, Nov 23, 2026): acceptance met by `docs/specs/messages.md` (connection model §4, consent §5, data model §7, reads §8, notices §9, sending through approvals §10, page and phone layout §11, calls §13, tests §17, questions §18, human items §19) and Phase B11 below (T-278 – T-293, all `proposed`); decisions D-66 – D-69 and human items H-078 – H-084 added.

- status: done (merged in PR #71, Nov 24, 2026)

TypeScript: `PROVIDERS` gains `microsoft_teams`; `connectRolesFor` returns `MAIL_CONNECT_ROLES`; `laneConfigSchema` gains the strict optional `teams` block (enabled default false, cloud, tenant_ids 1..10 lower-case unique and never the personal/nil tenant, allowed_domains 1..20, features with `chat_send` needing `chat_read` and `channels_send` needing `channels_read`; malformed refused) and a `teamsSettings(laneId)` reader that fails closed; `MODULES` gains `messages`, `NAV_OF` maps it, so a limited lane hides Messages unless listed. ### T-279 Teams: connect, guard, tokens, health, disconnect (connector, no chat data)
- status: done (merged in PR #71, Nov 24, 2026)
- depends: T-278
- needs: D-66
- acceptance: Spec §4.4, §5, §6. `packages/connectors/src/teams/`: OAuth provider (slug `teams`, provider `microsoft_teams`, env `GRAPH_TEAMS_CLIENT_ID/SECRET/REDIRECT_URI` through `requireConnectorEnv`, secrets in the redactor and the client-bundle canary; redirect path exactly `/api/connect/teams/callback`), start and precheck on `teams` settings only (never on `mail.providers`), scopes from the lane's features (§4.4 table), the Teams scope rule (every requested scope granted; an extra scope accepted only if on `TEAMS_KNOWN_SCOPES`; any `Mail.*` or other resource refuses; features = requested ∩ granted), consent errors -> `admin_consent_required`, id_token `tid`/`iss`/`aud`/`exp`/`nonce`, `/me` UPN + id + userType (guest refused), UPN domain in `teams.allowed_domains`, reconnect same UPN and tenant, D-23 and one-per-owner refusals with fixed messages, `tenant_id`/`cloud`/`entra_object_id` set once, audit. Guard (§6, including the explicit owner = session user check for user reads), token manager (rotation, CAS, single flight, no retried refresh), read and write HTTP clients with exact path allow-lists (the write client buildable only from the approvals wiring, lint + source test), Teams health check in `conn.token_health`, local disconnect (tokens, subscriptions marked removed, Teams rows of §7 deleted when they exist, open `teams.*` actions invalidated and purged; audited). Tests with fakes: acceptance tests 1 – 5 of spec §17; a Google-only lane gains a Teams connection without any Google row, job or setting changing; an NC-style lane keeps its Microsoft mail connect working after a Teams connect (no mail code path sees the new row); disallowed paths refused before a token read.

### T-281 Teams: chat reader and safe message rendering (connector)
- status: done (merged in PR #71, Nov 24, 2026)
- depends: T-279
- acceptance: Spec §8, §8.1, §8.3, §8.4. `TeamsReader.listChats` (combined `$expand=members,lastMessagePreview`, `$orderby` on the preview time, `$top=50`, opaque server-side cursor; fallback to preview-only plus parallel member reads when Graph refuses the combined expand), `listMembers`, `listMessages` (paging back by `createdDateTime lt`; new-since with the `lastModifiedDateTime` filter and orderby), strict projections with own/external flags and the unread rule; HTML -> typed safe nodes converter (allow-list of §8.4; https-only links; mentions, emoji and images as text/placeholders; no raw HTML out); attachment stubs (name text, https link, never fetched). Tests: a hostile-HTML corpus (script, style, iframe, svg, event attributes, javascript:/data: links, external img, nested tricks) renders safely; unread cases (newer preview from another user, from self, hidden chat, portal marker newer); a canary in preview, body, topic and member names never reaches a DB write, logger, audit, event or error (spies); 429 with Retry-After on GETs; per-user and per-chat pacing; ids validated before any request.

### T-283 Messages page: chat list and conversation (desktop and iPhone)
- status: done (merged in PR #71, Nov 24, 2026)
- depends: T-280, T-281, T-282
- acceptance: Spec §8.1, §11. `/[laneId]/messages` with `?chat=<b64url>`: desktop two panes, phone list then full-screen conversation with back; rows with title, preview, ET time, unread dot, External label; conversation with Load earlier, day separators, senders, Edited/Deleted markers, attachment stubs, image placeholders, Refresh, Open in Teams; opening a chat records the read marker. Every state of the §11 table; never an empty list on an error. Performance (T-276): shell and skeleton at once; lane gate, `teamsSettings` and the own Teams row in parallel from shared request reads; list and conversation Graph reads started together in separate Suspense sections; no Graph call on a link prefetch (test); no other page gains a read. Server render tests with synthetic chats for each state at desktop and iPhone width; privacy test (an admin passing another person's connection id gets nothing, no Graph call).

### T-284 Messages: Call, Video call and Open in Teams buttons (deep links)
- status: done (merged in PR #71, Nov 24, 2026)
- depends: T-283
- acceptance: Spec §13. `POST /api/[laneId]/messages/call-link` `{chatKey, video}`: owner and feature `call_links` checked; same-tenant members other than the person resolved to UPNs (`GET /users/{id}?$select=userPrincipalName`) in memory only; returns `https://teams.microsoft.com/l/call/0/0?users=...` (`&withVideo=true` for video); refused when any other member is external or there are more than 10. Buttons shown only when allowed, otherwise Open in Teams (chat `webUrl` with host check, or the `/l/chat/<chatId>/conversations` link); channels get Open in Teams only. Links open in a new tab; nothing stored or logged. Tests: 1:1, group of 3, external member, 11 members, missing feature ("Reconnect Teams to add call buttons"), a `webUrl` on another host ignored.

### T-285 Messages: inline images through a server proxy
- status: done (merged in PR #72, Nov 24, 2026)
- depends: T-283
- acceptance: Spec §8.5. `/api/lanes/<lane>/messages/image` streams hosted content of a message the guard and owner check allow (chat now; channels with T-291): image/png|jpeg|gif|webp only, 4 MB cap (stream aborted beyond), `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `Content-Disposition: inline`, `nosniff`, `Content-Security-Policy: sandbox`; never stored or logged; the converter's image placeholders become `<img>` to this route only for hosted content of the same message. Tests: wrong content type, oversize, another person's connection, another lane, an id not in that message.

### T-286 Approvals: Teams action types in SQL

Production SQL part and check.

### T-287 Teams writer and reconciler (connector write side)
- status: done (merged in PR #72, Nov 24, 2026)
- depends: T-281, T-286
- acceptance: Spec §10.4. `prepareTeamsWrite` and `executeWrite` behind the approvals-only brand: guard, the full `prepare` re-checks, ONE `POST` with `{"body":{"contentType":"text","content":...}}` and `retry: false`; read-back by id (sender = `entra_object_id`, same chat/channel/parent, after the claim, normalised text equal; logs `teams.send.read_back_form` = text|html, never text); the outcome table of §10.4 (201 verified / sent_unverified; 400/403/404/413 failed with fixed messages; 429 failed, not retried; timeout/5xx/network -> date-range lookup: one match verified `already_applied`, two `ambiguous_match`, none stays executing); `TeamsMessageReconciler` (find + verify only) registered in `action.reconcile_stuck`. Tests with fakes for every row, a forbidden path or method refused before a token read, and no text in logs, audit or errors.

### T-288 Messages: Send = your approval (composer, route, one user step)
- status: done (merged in PR #73, Nov 24, 2026; first live send confirmed by Saurabh the same day)
- depends: T-283, T-287, T-295 (added Nov 24, 2026: build after the several-accounts work so the composer and route are built once against it, spec §22.8), T-297 (the composer goes into the pinned place T-297 reserves at the bottom of the conversation pane)
- decisions: D-67 answered Nov 24, 2026: "Once per 8 hours (Recommended)" (28,800 s for `teams.*` sends).
- acceptance: Spec §10.3, §10.5, §22.8. **Step-up (D-67):** the route calls `requireStepUp(facts.teamsStepUpMaxAgeS)`; 28,800 s is inside the setting's clamp (300 – 43,200: lane_config schema, `act.approve`, `teamsStepUpFromConfig`), but `requireStepUp` in `packages/core/src/auth/index.ts:187` refuses any window above 3,600 s (`forbidden`), so this task widens that guard's upper bound to 43,200 (or adds a Teams-only variant with that bound) while every existing caller keeps its own smaller value (email stays 30 – 900); auth code: full security review, tests for 3,600, 28,800, 43,200 and 43,201 and that email approvals are unchanged. The builder also writes the runbook lines and a gitignored SQL pair for a lane's Teams window: one insert-only lane_config version setting `approvals.teams_step_up_max_age_s` (28,800 for PKG in H-082), every other key unchanged, refusing if the value is already set; a read-only check file asserting the new value and every other key equal to the previous version's. **Several accounts (§22.8):** the composer belongs to the conversation opened through `conn`; the send body's `connectionId` is that `conn`, re-checked like every Messages route (§22.4: the caller's own usable Teams row in this lane, else 404 before anything is proposed) and again by the approvals SQL; the line reads "Send posts this exact text to Teams as <UPN of that connection> (logged)" (also with one account); a chat both accounts are in is two rows (D-70), so each composer belongs to one account and the other account's row has its own empty composer; Approvals history shows "Teams message (chat) · <account label>"; a test sends on the second of two connections and proves the action, the POST and the read-back use that connection only, and a send naming another person's or another lane's connection is refused with no proposal. `sendTeamsMessage` in `packages/modules/approvals` and `POST /api/[laneId]/messages/send`: AAL2, step-up per D-67, module visible, owner, `teams.enabled` + `chat_send`, `teams_send`, `approvals_live` (refusal names the gate), `textSha256` from the browser equals the server's, propose + approve with the computed hash + claim + execute + verify inline; status history and audit show the person's approval (ids, type, hash; never text); 1 request/s per connection. Composer: plain text, counter, "Send posts this exact text to Teams as you (logged)", Ctrl/Cmd+Enter on desktop, button only on phone; bubble states Sending / Sent / Sent, checking / Not sent (text kept) / Outcome unknown; Approvals history lists "Teams message" without text. Tests: spec §17 tests 10 – 13, double click, hash mismatch, render tests of each bubble state at both widths.

### T-289 Teams: new-message notices (subscriptions, receiver, activity)

SQL: `comms.teams_activity` (exactly the listed columns, owner-only RLS, `teams_activity_notice` service-only, `teams_activity_seen` owner-only, deleted on disconnect; pgTAP incl. cross-lane/cross-user); `core.push_subscriptions.resource` accepts `teams_chats`. Connector: Teams subscriptions for `users/<entra_object_id>/chats/getAllMessages`, `changeType` `created`, never `includeResourceData`, both URLs from `GRAPH_NOTIFICATION_URL`, Vault clientState, 4,200 minutes asked and Microsoft's answer recorded; an answer under 26 hours stops it (`teams.subscription.lifetime_too_short`); a refused resource -> `teams.subscription.unsupported`, no per-chat fallback; renew within 24 h by `conn.subscriptions_renew` (lists usable Teams connections of lanes with `teams.enabled` + `chat_read`), stop when paused or owner lost `own_mail`. Receiver logic unchanged (a test: a `teams_chats` row yields `teams_chats.changed`, `resource` never read, no Graph call); new Inngest function `teams-activity` records the notice; `microsoft-webhook-sync` skips it. Tests with fake clocks and fakes (spec §17 tests 14, 15). Production SQL part and check.

## Phase B12 — Messages: several Teams accounts per person (Saurabh, Nov 24, 2026)
Requested by Saurabh on Nov 24, 2026, after the first live Teams test in production, verbatim: "I logged in, and I can see the Teams messages inside the portal. I was able to connect using the pubkgroup.com account. What I want is another connection for Teams. As you know, we have got two different teams for pubkgroup.com and pub-k.org, so I need another option, especially under Pub K group, to have the second team connected. Those messages, when they are displayed in the portal, can be combined together." The planner wrote the tasks as `proposed`. Recorded by the lead agent on Nov 24, 2026: T-294 – T-297 and T-288 are set to `approved` on the strength of Saurabh's own requests of that day (the one quoted above for the second account; "look at the snapshot of the design. Can you just improve it a bit and make it tidier, especially the right-side window" for T-297; "I do not see the option to send messages from the portal. I can only read the messages. Can you also check that?" plus his D-67 answer for T-288), in the same way his earlier feature requests were built. He was told in the same conversation that his message was taken as the go-ahead; if he says otherwise, building stops.

Spec: `docs/specs/messages.md` §22 (answers Q-M10). Decision D-70 (a chat both accounts are in: default chosen by the lead agent Nov 24, 2026 and told to Saurabh, two rows, one per account; merging later stays open; nothing waits on it). Human items H-085 (production part 37, the PKG limit, connecting the second account, live checks A-1 – A-4). Every task is app code (RLS/data access, connectors/tokens): full security + code review, fix all BLOCKER and HIGH. Synthetic data and MSW fakes only. Generic for every lane: the number is `lane_config.teams.max_accounts_per_person` (default 1); no domain, tenant or company in code.

**Build order:** T-294 → T-295 → T-296 → T-297 → T-288 → T-292, one builder at a time. Why T-288 last of these: it builds the composer, the send route and the "posts as" line; built after T-295 it is written once against the `conn` addressing (the send body's `connectionId` is the open conversation's `conn`) and names the right account; built after T-297 it drops into the pinned composer place T-297 reserves at the bottom of the conversation pane, so the pane layout is done once. T-297 comes after T-296 because both change the same Messages component files (T-296 the list layout, T-297 the conversation pane). D-67 is answered (Nov 24, 2026), so nothing else blocks T-288.

### T-294 Teams: several accounts per person — SQL, lane setting, connect and Settings
- status: done (merged in PR #72, Nov 24, 2026)
- depends: T-278, T-279, T-280, T-289 (production part 37 comes after part 36)
- decisions: D-70 does not affect this task.
- note: requested by Saurabh, Nov 24, 2026 (quoted in the phase header). Approval recorded by the lead agent from that request (see the phase header).
- acceptance: Spec §22.1, §22.2, §22.3, §22.10. **SQL** (migration + pgTAP in the same change): drop `core.connections_one_teams_per_owner`; new `core.teams_account_limit(lane)` (current lane_config `teams.max_accounts_per_person`, 1 – 5, else 1; service_role only); `core.teams_assert_one_per_owner` body replaced (name, signature, grants and codes kept; `D0024` when making this connection usable would exceed the limit; an already counted row passes); new trigger `connections_teams_account_limit` on `core.connections` (Teams rows; checks only a move into a counted status; advisory lock per lane and owner; `D0024`); `core.connection_token_store`, `core.connection_set_teams_identity`, `core.teams_connection_ids`, both unique indexes and every RLS policy unchanged (pgTAP proves they still hold). No data change: the live PKG row stays valid. pgTAP: spec §22.12 tests 22 – 26 (limit, default and malformed limit, cross-user and cross-lane denial for every changed function and the trigger, uniqueness kept, only moves checked, concurrency, approvals on a second connection); 328 updated where it asserted one per owner. **Production:** gitignored `part-37-T-294-precheck.sql` (read-only: part 36 present, md5 of the replaced body and of the relied-on `connection_set_teams_identity` and `connection_token_store`, the index to drop with its exact definition, the kept indexes, the new objects absent; every `ok` true), `part-37-T-294.sql` (one transaction; refuses a second run, a missing part 36 or any differing fact), `part-37-T-294-check.sql` (read-only, every row true); exact row counts in H-085. **Lane config:** strict `teams.max_accounts_per_person` (int 1 – 5, default 1) in the schema, settings reader and settings view; runbook template and its test; a runbook section "Allow several Teams accounts per person" with a gitignored SQL pair (one insert-only lane_config version setting the key, every other key unchanged, refusing if the key exists; check file asserting the new value and every other key equal to the previous version's) and the warning that it runs only after this code is deployed. **Connector and connect:** start refuses at the limit (`one_account`, new fixed `TEAMS_ONE_ACCOUNT_MESSAGE`) before redirecting; callback refuses via the function and the trigger with nothing stored; the same account chosen again refreshes its own row and reports `already_connected` (Teams provider only; Google banners unchanged); reconnect of one row unchanged (`login_hint`, `wrong_account` with the row's UPN in the banner); Teams harness with two fake accounts of one fake tenant enforcing the limit instead of one per person. **Settings:** "Your Teams accounts", one row per own connection, Reconnect / Disconnect per row, "Connect another Teams account" while under the limit, the at-limit note, reconnect of a disconnected row only while under the limit, the new banners; admins see health rows only. The Messages page keeps working with two usable rows (it shows the first until T-295). **Tests:** §22.12 tests 22 – 28 and the Settings part of 33; disconnect of one leaves the other's tokens, subscription, markers and counters (test); Google and Microsoft mail tests unchanged and passing.

### T-295 Messages: one combined chat list for several accounts (routes, page, notices)
- status: done (merged in PR #72, Nov 24, 2026)
- depends: T-294, T-283, T-284, T-285, T-290
- decisions: D-70 (default chosen Nov 24, 2026 and built: a chat both accounts are in is two rows, one per account; no merging, no account switching).
- note: requested by Saurabh, Nov 24, 2026 ("Those messages, when they are displayed in the portal, can be combined together"). Approval recorded by the lead agent from that request (see the phase header).
- acceptance: Spec §22.4 – §22.8 (the chat parts). **Addressing:** `conn` (the person's own connection id) on the page and on `chats`, `older`, `newer`, `read`, `call-link`, `image` (chat form) and the optional `conns` body of "seen", with the check order of §22.4 (uuid → own row in THIS lane under RLS → usable → feature → guard owner check); another person's, another lane's, disconnected, pending and unknown ids get the same 404 with zero Graph requests and zero token reads; `?chat=` without `conn` opens a chat only for a person with one usable connection. **State model:** `messagesStateOf` returns every own account (label, state, missing features); "Connected as" lists them with a "Connect another Teams account" link while under the limit; per-account feature and reconnect notes. **Combined list:** one `listChats` per usable account with `chat_read`, started together with the conversation read; merged by preview time with the tie rule; account badge (text; domain or local part; accessible name with the UPN; 14-character truncation at iPhone width; only with two or more accounts); a chat both accounts are in shown as two rows, one per account, each with its own label, unread marker and `conn` (D-70 default; rows keyed by (connection id, chat id); no "+1", no "View as", no switching); the combined "More chats" cursor (`v:1`, ≤ 5 entries, ≤ 6,400 characters, every entry re-checked, foreign entries dropped with the fixed line); a (connection, chat) row already shown not drawn twice; one markers query for all accounts; partial failure lines per account (reconnect with that row's Reconnect, busy with seconds, unreachable with Try again), the failure card only when all fail. **Counts:** member-fallback budget of 20 in total; notices re-read only the account whose `lastNoticeAt` moved; per-person limiters unchanged. **Notices:** activity GET answers the aggregate plus `accounts[]`; the dot when any account has an unseen notice; "seen" posted for the accounts whose list loaded; each Settings row's live state from its own entry (one request). Call / Video call and pictures use the chat's `conn`. **Tests:** §22.12 tests 29 – 32 and the Messages part of 33 (desktop and 375 px), section 17 tests 6 and 8 with two accounts per person, prefetch still makes no Graph request.

### T-296 Messages: channels for several accounts, and the chat list kept mounted across chat changes
- status: done (merged in PR #72, Nov 24, 2026)
- depends: T-295, T-291
- decisions: D-70 (the same default for a team both accounts belong to: listed under each account).
- note: requested by Saurabh, Nov 24, 2026 (quoted in the phase header); the chat-list part is the open follow-up C5 of the T-281 review (`docs/plan-review.md`, "T-281 – T-284 review follow-up"), which doubles in cost with two accounts. Approval recorded by the lead agent from that request (see the phase header).
- acceptance: Spec §22.6 (chat clicks), §22.9. **Channels:** `joinedTeams` per account with `channels_read`, in parallel, grouped under the account label (two or more accounts); a team both accounts belong to listed under each account's group, each entry reading and posting as its own account (D-70 default; no switching); channels, posts, replies and channel pictures read only through the team's `conn` (in the address and the image route's channel form); per-account `channels_read` / admin-consent state; the shared-channel and archived rules of T-291 unchanged. **Chat list kept mounted:** a segment layout or parallel slot renders the combined list once; a change of only `?conn=` / `?chat=` does not re-read any list (`navigation-levels.test.ts` asserts it); the selected-row highlight and the opened chat's dot come from the client; the module gate, the owner checks and "no chat data in the prefetched shell" kept; a `?more=` change no longer re-reads the open conversation if the layout allows it (else logged as remaining). **Tests:** channel reads with two fake accounts (addressing denials as test 29, counts, a shared team), render tests at desktop and 375 px, the navigation test above.

### T-297 Messages: a tidier conversation pane (right side)
- status: done (merged in PR #72, Nov 24, 2026)
- depends: T-296 (same component files)
- note: Saurabh, Nov 24, 2026, verbatim: "look at the snapshot of the design. Can you just improve it a bit and make it tidier, especially the right-side window". The right-side window is the conversation pane of Messages (`components/messages-chats.tsx` `ConversationPane`, `components/messages-client.tsx`, `components/messages-page.tsx`). Designs are approved (D-21); this is polish within them. **What the screenshot (H-086; wide desktop window; real chats, not stored, nothing copied) shows:** (a) a large "Connected as <account>" card with a second line and a Settings link takes a full block above the two panes; (b) the conversation header uses four stacked rows: the chat name (small serif), a people line ("2 people, includes external people"), "Open in Teams" alone on its own row, and a sentence that calls from here are only for people in your own organisation; Refresh sits alone at the top right; (c) every message has its time on its own line under the bubble, so a run of short messages from one person is mostly white space; no day separators, no grouping; (d) incoming bubbles are white with a border on a white pane and stretch to about three quarters of the pane; own bubbles are grey with a border, right-aligned: own and incoming are hard to tell apart; (e) a long incoming message ends with several blank lines inside the bubble (trailing empty paragraphs from Teams' HTML); (f) the chat list and the conversation run past the bottom of the window: the whole page scrolls instead of each pane.
- acceptance: Generic, design tokens only, no new dependency; no new server read or request; navigation-level tests unchanged; nothing from the screenshot in code, tests or fixtures (synthetic data only). **Connection line:** the "Connected as" card becomes one slim row (the account, or with several accounts (spec §22) their labels, and the link to Settings); connect states that need action (not connected, reconnect, feature missing, paused, unavailable) keep their full card. **Header:** one compact row: chat name and people line on the left, actions (Call, Video call, Open in Teams, Refresh) on the right; the calls note as small muted text under it only when calls are unavailable for this chat; wraps cleanly at iPhone width (375 px) with 44 px touch targets. **Messages:** consecutive messages of one sender within 5 minutes form a group with one time shown for the group (each message's full time stays available to screen readers and on hover/tap); a day separator between days (lane time zone, the existing time helpers); in group chats the sender name once per group; bubble width capped (about 65 characters of text, never wider than the pane minus a margin); own messages use a distinct token-based tint with sufficient contrast (WCAG AA) in both themes, not grey; spacing tightened. **Converter:** the HTML-to-nodes converter (`packages/connectors/src/teams/message-html.ts`) trims leading and trailing empty blocks and collapses runs of empty paragraphs and line breaks to one, in the typed-node output (still no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`); tests with hostile and empty inputs (only empty paragraphs, only `<br>`, whitespace, nested empty blocks). **Layout:** on desktop the list and the conversation each fill the remaining viewport height and scroll independently (the page itself does not scroll); the conversation opens scrolled to the newest message; a pinned place at the bottom of the conversation pane is reserved for the composer T-288 adds (messages scroll above it); iPhone: the existing one-pane behaviour, full height with the safe areas, nothing hidden behind the tab bar. **Tests:** render tests at desktop and iPhone width for a 1:1 chat, a group chat, a long message, a run of short messages across two days, and the empty conversation; accessibility: reading order, labels of the header actions, focus stays on Refresh after refreshing; before/after screenshots with synthetic data in the PR body. App code (UI and the converter): code review; security review of the converter change.

### T-299 Response headers against framing and content sniffing (finding F-01 of the security suite)
- status: done (merged in PR #75, Nov 25, 2026)
- depends: T-170
- note: approved by Saurabh, Nov 24, 2026 ("yes", in reply to the lead agent's question whether to build the header fix as its own small task). Finding F-01 of `docs/security/pilot-test.md`: the app sends no `frame-ancestors`, `X-Frame-Options` or `X-Content-Type-Options`; rated MEDIUM while the portal has its own site (the session cookie is SameSite=Lax), HIGH if it ever shares a registrable domain with pages not fully controlled. Approvals are live for PKG (D-71), and a Teams Send is one click within the 8-hour window.
- acceptance: every response of the web app (pages, API routes, the error and not-found pages) carries `X-Frame-Options: DENY`, `Content-Security-Policy: frame-ancestors 'none'` and `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, set in one place (`apps/web/next.config.ts` `headers()` or `proxy.ts`, whichever also covers API routes and static files); the existing `Referrer-Policy` stays; routes that set their own stricter `Content-Security-Policy` (the Teams image route's `sandbox`) keep it and still forbid framing; no script or style policy is added in this task (a full CSP is a separate decision: record it in docs/plan-review.md); nothing the app does legitimately breaks (the installed iPhone app, OAuth redirects, the Inngest and webhook endpoints, images); the `it.fails` pin for F-01 in `apps/web/tests/security-headers.test.ts` becomes a passing test and is extended to assert the headers on a page, an API route and the image route; the finding is closed in `docs/security/pilot-test.md` with the human step "confirm the headers in production with the browser's network panel". App code touching every response: security review and code review, fix BLOCKER and HIGH.


## Phase B13 — Finance: the controller's reports (Saurabh, Nov 27, 2026)

Request (Saurabh, Nov 27, 2026, verbatim): "here are the reports being published by our finance controller. whats the best way to show them on the finance section. please review and advise before building — Cash report; Aged AR report; Invoices and received payments by week". No sample file or screenshot came with the message; the layouts are not known. **Advice first, nothing built.** The three tasks below are placeholders so the request is on record; the planner writes the acceptance once Saurabh has sent the layouts and answered the questions the lead agent asked the same day (live figures or the controller's file; what the cash report contains; number of weeks; whether credit memos and sales receipts count).

Facts found Nov 27 (code and migrations, not production): the Finance page is one page (KPI cards, Subscriptions, Receivables aging by bucket, Cash by account, Controls, a list of cached reports). Stored from QuickBooks: accounts with the current book balance of bank accounts (no history), invoices (customer name, issue date, due date, total, balance; no document number), payments (date, amount, one row per invoice it was applied to), bills, transaction lines of Purchase, Deposit, Payment, BillPayment, Bill and JournalEntry. Not synced: SalesReceipt, CreditMemo, RefundReceipt, Transfer; payroll is excluded (D-25). Balance Sheet and Cash Flow are not cached (D-27). H-024 (the live PKG QuickBooks file) is unchecked.

**Answers (Saurabh, Nov 27, 2026, verbatim): "I dont have snapshot of the report and live pub k quickbooks is already connected...you can go like 4 weeks back. Yes I agree computng directly from quickbooks".** Taken as the go-ahead to build (he opened with "now we need to build some reports from quickbooks"). So: figures computed live from the synced QuickBooks data; no sample layouts exist, so the layouts are the lead agent's (standard controller layouts, spec `docs/specs/finance.md` §15); 4 completed business weeks plus the current week to date. Not answered, the lead agent's defaults (he was told): the cash report shows balances only, no money in and out; aging by due date; no invoice numbers. H-024 (live PKG file connected) is his statement, not checked by the agent; its box is left as it is.

### T-300 Finance: tabs and Aged AR by customer
- status: done (true on merge; built and reviewed Nov 27, 2026 on `agent/T-300`; approved by Saurabh, Nov 27, 2026, see the phase note; production steps H-088)
- depends: T-243
- acceptance: spec `docs/specs/finance.md` §15.1 and §15.2. Finance gets a tab row (Overview, Receivables, Invoices & payments; Cash is added by T-302); Overview is today's page unchanged. Receivables: one row per customer with the five buckets (Current, 1–30, 31–60, 61–90, 90+, by days past due) and a total, a totals row, the open invoices of each customer on expansion (invoice date, due date, days overdue, balance), "as of" and source, and a line comparing the computed total with the cached QuickBooks AR aging summary when that total is readable. Same open-invoice set as `core.home_finance_detail` (a pgTAP test proves the bucket totals are equal). Every figure from SQL (rule 7); `laneId` everywhere; finance view and AAL2 as the Overview; a cross-lane denial test; production SQL as a manual part with a check.

### T-301 Finance: invoices and received payments by week
- status: done (true on merge; built and reviewed Nov 27, 2026 on `agent/T-300`; approved by Saurabh, Nov 27, 2026, see the phase note; production steps H-088)
- depends: T-300
- acceptance: spec §15.3. One row per business week (Sat–Fri, America/New_York, rule 11): the current week to date and the completed weeks before it (`lane_config.finance.reports.weeks`, 1 to 13, default 4): invoices issued (count, amount) and payments received (count, amount), a grouped bar chart, and the invoices and payments of a chosen week. A payment counts once whatever the number of invoices it was applied to. Voided and deleted rows are left out. A boundary test: Friday and Saturday dates fall in the right weeks. The page says what is not included (sales receipts, credit memos, refunds: not synced). Built with T-300 in one branch and one PR.

### T-302 Finance: cash report (book balance per bank account by week)
- status: done (true on merge; built and reviewed Nov 27, 2026 on `agent/T-302`; approved by Saurabh, Nov 27, 2026, see the phase note; as built the weekly history is OFF by default and enabled per lane, spec §15.4 "As built"; production steps H-089)
- depends: T-301
- acceptance: spec §15.4. Cash tab: the book balance of each bank account today and at the end of each of the last completed business weeks (same number of weeks as T-301), the change from week to week, a total row, "as of" per column, every figure labelled "book balance" (rule 7). Past balances come from a QuickBooks Balance Sheet read at each week-end date of which ONLY the rows of the lane's bank accounts are kept (no total, equity, net income or any other row is stored; D-27 stays as it is, and the security review must confirm that nothing payroll-related can be derived). New storage has `lane_id`, RLS and pgTAP with a cross-lane denial test in the same change. No money in and out in this version (transfers, sales receipts and payroll are not synced, so they would not add up). Full security and code review (connector and data access).
